SELECT COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`present`), 0) AS `present`, COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`absent`), 0) AS `absent`, COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`late`), 0) AS `late`, COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`leave_count`), 0) AS `leave_count`, COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`underTime`), 0) AS `underTime`, @__year_1 AS `year`, CAST((COALESCE(SUM(`t`.`present`), 0) / 365) * 100 AS decimal(65,30)) AS `attendanceRate`

) AS `t`
